# Campaign fresh gravity — the ledger

One headline per committed lane, in the charter's convention: constants counted as fitted / declared / tied / derived;
both a₀ footings; κ = ½ fitted; nothing here says the theory is closed. Each lane's README holds the numbers and the files.

| Lane | Headline | Standing |
|---|---|---|
| CFG0 | 81 own findings inventoried. No constant was reduced beyond λ becoming a regulator. The 74–89% cluster claim is withdrawn. | inventory |
| CFG1 | Evidence audit: 14 model-independent, 11 soft, 8 contested results. X-COP is soft through the hydrostatic bias. 12 past verdicts rest on the record's own approximations. | audit |
| CFG4 | The target law: one effective description fits every model-independent fact in a narrow window. It needs a density edge at x_e ≈ 0.31–0.46 r_ta, the max rule (T5) and a bound-only switch. The minimal conflict is KiDS reach against the cold budget. | target |
| CFG6 | Keep flat a₀. Carry the leaf-averaged √ρ_DE tie as one labelled variant. | branch choice |
| CFG7 · FG041 | Tidal dwarfs are Newtonian (χ² 1.09 for 6). The framework's law with the host's field is worse by Δχ² +5.2 to +12.0; the isolated law is excluded. **FG001's own kill test passed.** | pass |
| CFG7 · FG001 | Hierarchical ownership of the phantom, with T5's retained cold component. It passes 9/14 of the record's population gates, against 6/14 for the law with the external field. ξ is retired. Its cost is Chae's external-field fits (4.1/4.3σ). Two sub-hypotheses failed (globular clusters do not discriminate; M31 LVD at 2.2–2.6σ). | constructive; −1 constant (ξ) |
| CFG7 · FG004 | The max rule's outer part is the relaxed (isothermal) state: σ² = V_f²/2, and P = a₀g_N/(8πG) is derived. The inner part is not: a relaxed cold component overshoots by +0.13–0.15 dex at g_bar > a₀. That locates the next ingredient. | half derived |
| CFG7 · FG016 | Killed as written. The splashback edge at the law's own accretion rate lands at x_e = 0.18–0.27, below [0.31, 0.48]. KiDS with the derived profile fails by Δχ² +50 to +95. The budget holds. It sharpens CFG4's conflict to "KiDS against the collapse's cold supply"; the hybrid fails CMB lensing. | failed; x_e stays declared |
| CFG7 · FG097 | The harness. B (CFG4 + FG001) scores 38/48, against 32/48 for CFG4 as committed and for the external-field rival; C 34/48 (KiDS); F 36/48 (CMB lensing). It flags lanes whose own controls failed. | infrastructure |
| CFG8 | FG001's one cost, Chae's external-field signal, refit on his 153 galaxies with his priors (his own numbers reproduced). Under the framework's own law at the canonical a₀ the median fitted field drops from 3.1σ to 1.7σ (P2) or 2.2σ (ν_mono); the alt footing leaves 2.7–3.0σ. His low-acceleration RAR downturn (−0.076 dex) vanishes under P2 at the canonical a₀ (−0.007 ± 0.007). Under no law does the fitted field rank-correlate with his environmental field (p 0.57–0.87); the weighted slope rests on a few precise galaxies (NGC 5055, NGC 5033), whose declines persist under every law. H1 failed as declared. | cost reduced, not removed |
| CFG9 | The kernel's shape from local virial equilibrium. Around a point mass, a cold component with σ² = V_c²/2 at every radius makes the field exactly P2, with a₀ = 4π × its charge per unit baryonic mass; equivalently 4πr²P = a₀M/2. P2 is the only such law (the ν_β family and ν_mono depart). Inside real baryons the law's phantom is isothermal at the flat speed (FG004 reproduced to 0.004) but about 70% hotter than local virial at g_bar > a₀. A constant-charge component overshoots the law there by +0.06–0.09 dex; a charge tracking the enclosed baryons matches P2's SPARC rms within 0.003 dex. The principle picks P2, not the contract kernel ν_mono, which fits SPARC 0.004–0.008 dex better. | shape derived for a point mass; inner open |
| CFG10 | What sets the cold component inside the baryons. Two zero-constant principles, each exact for a point mass, scored with the record's SPARC statistic at their own best Υ. The pressure Gauss law (4πr²P = a₀M_b(<r)/2) is **excluded**: +0.17 dex, because it demands negative cold density at a quarter of the radii in 116 of 175 galaxies. The shell theorem for the charge (ρ_c g = (a₀/3) ρ̄_b(<r)) **passes**: within 0.002–0.003 dex of P2, with the same inner residual. P2 equals it plus a non-negative local term that tracks the baryon density (an exact identity). FG004's missing "inner mechanism" is not a separate ingredient: its overshoot came from assuming a constant charge. | inner law carried by one statement; its dynamics open |
| CFG11 | The cold budget under FG001's own hierarchy (Kourkchi & Tully 2017 groups). Only top-level systems own a phantom, and because the phantom is sublinear in M_b, grouping cuts the budget by 11–23% (R = 0.77–0.89). On the canonical footing that opens CFG4's strict window in the deeper samples (edge 0.32–0.34 against the KiDS floor of 0.31); the alt footing stays closed (0.27–0.29) and would need R ≈ 0.72–0.78. H1 failed as declared. Counting turned-around associations as the top-level system is untested. | conflict narrowed, not closed |
| CFG12 | CFG4's strict budget compared all galaxies' phantoms, down to M_* = 1e7, with the turned-around share at one scale (5.45e11 M☉, 0.602). The consistent share is the one above the smallest budgeted system's turnaround mass (1.1e11): 0.667–0.672. On the canonical footing the strict window then opens (edge 0.312 against 0.309; with FG001's grouping, 0.34). The alt footing stays ~5% short (0.29–0.30 against 0.31). The mass-resolved budget binds at the smallest scale, so the global form is the right one. H1/H2 (both footings) failed and are kept. | canonical conflict resolved; alt 5% short |
| CFG13 | CFG9's principle on its own terms. At SPARC points where the baryons look like a point mass (878 points), the transition shape gives β̂ = 0.72 / 0.88; the 95% intervals, [0.48, 6] and [0.63, 6], do not exclude P2's β = 1. **The bias control failed:** CFG4's shape estimator returns β ≈ 0.55–0.59 for data that are P2 by construction, so CFG4's exclusion of P2 (β = 0.48) is under review (CFG14). H2 failed (the embedded subset excludes 1 only on canonical). | principle not falsified; estimator biased |
| CFG14 | The kernel-shape estimator, calibrated with 60 noise realizations per truth and two declared noise models. The estimator is median-unbiased, but CFG4's galaxy bootstrap under-covers its spread by about 5×. The real β̂ (0.48 / 0.55) sits in P2's tail at p ≈ 0.03 (canonical, both models; alt model A) and p = 0.13 (alt model B), and well inside ν_mono's (p 0.57–1.0). So P2 applied everywhere is disfavoured at about 2σ, not excluded at >99.9%. CFG13's "biased low" reading is withdrawn: that was one low draw. The point-mass points do not discriminate; the preference comes from inside the baryons. H1 failed as declared. | CFG4 H3 overstated; errata in CFG4/9/13 |
| CFG15 | Does CFG12's canonical window survive a physical 2-halo amplitude? The KiDS floor used A ≤ 2, and A is the lens bias; the fit takes 1.7–1.9 in the massive bins. At the framework's own peak-background bias (1.1–1.6) the floor is 0.317 / 0.322, so the window survives: [0.317, 0.341]. With unbiased lenses (A ≤ 1) it is 0.350 / 0.360 and the window closes by 0.01–0.02. CFG12's "resolved" is downgraded to marginal and bias-dependent; the true bias of isolated lenses at the truncated masses is lower than 1.1–1.6. H2 failed as declared. | CFG12 downgraded |
| CFG16 | The self-consistent KiDS floor: each lens bin's 2-halo cap is the peak-background bias of the truncated profile's own turnaround mass (0.9–1.2, an upper estimate that ignores isolation). The floor is 0.341 / 0.348 against CFG12's edge of 0.341 / 0.338, so the canonical window closes: P2 by 0.0003, ν_mono by 0.010. Alt is closed by about 0.05. CFG4's minimal conflict (KiDS reach against the cold budget) is not resolved; it sits at about 3% (canonical) and 15% (alt). H1 failed as declared. | conflict stands, at the margin |
| CFG17 | The cold budget with a stellar-mass-selected gas relation (xGASS, 1179 galaxies, data committed with provenance). SPARC over-states the gas only above about 10^10.3 M☉, so the phantom budget falls 5–8% there and the edge moves up by about 0.008. Against CFG16's self-consistent KiDS floor, canonical P2 opens a window [0.341, 0.348] and canonical ν_mono is closed by 0.0004–0.0018; alt stays closed by about 12%. The canonical conflict is at the margin (±2%) and not decidable at this precision. H1 and H2 failed as declared. | canonical at the margin; alt closed |
| CFG18 | FG001's satellites scored with their infall baryons, as FG001's class A says, instead of their stripped present-day stars. The infall gas is drawn from 42 LVD field dwarfs of the same stellar mass (HI detections and upper limits, spread propagated, no extrapolation). The M31 LVD dwarfs move from 2.6σ / 2.2σ to 1.6σ / 1.2σ, Collins from 2.5σ to 1.4σ, and the MW classical dSphs to 0.4σ, so H1 passes. The MW ultra-faints still fail at 7–8σ. This is a robustness test of FG001's exploratory H8, not blind. | FG001's M31 tension removed; UFDs remain |
| CFG19 | The harness re-scored with CFG18. Candidate B (CFG4's target plus FG001) goes from 38/48 to **42/48**: the four M31 satellite gates flip on both footings. It still fails the MW ultra-faints and Chae's D1/D2 gates, which are FG001's committed values; CFG8's different statistic is not substituted. The external-field rival stays at 32/48. | B 42/48 |
| CFG20 | The Local Group against the target law's edge. Under FG001 the LG is top-level with no external field; the untruncated law gives R₀ = 1.92–2.02 Mpc against the observed 0.93 ± 0.12. Truncating the phantom at KiDS's self-consistent floor (x_e ≈ 0.34) brings R₀ to 1.28–1.51 Mpc, still 3.0–4.8σ high; the LG allows x_e ≤ 0.16–0.26. **This is a new conflict: KiDS's large-radius signal against the LG timing mass.** It joins the budget (at the margin) and FG016's splashback edge (0.18–0.27), which also want the phantom shorter. H1 failed. C1b failed by 2.7e-6 Mpc (FP1's interpolation table; C1c shows it). | new conflict: KiDS vs LG |
| CFG21 | One number for the edge conflict. KiDS's isolated lenses alone prefer x_e ≈ 0.62 (Δχ² −35 to −38 against the untruncated law); the Local Group alone prefers 0.10–0.13. With one shared edge the parameter-difference tension is **5.1–5.3σ** (canonical, LG 1.145e11) and 5.6–7.1σ otherwise. A universal sharp edge is excluded. The statistic was revised after the MUTATE run and before any main run, because the first-written one passed trivially; the revision is disclosed. | sharp edge excluded 5–7σ |
| CFG22 | A soft edge: inside r_s = x_s r_ta the phantom follows the law; beyond it continues as ρ ∝ r⁻³ out to r_ta. It makes the conflict **worse**: T_min is 29.8–54.0 (5.5–7.4σ) against the sharp edge's 26.2–50.6. The tail supplies KiDS but piles mass inside the LG's R₀. The LG caps the total mass within ~1 Mpc of MW+M31 near 3 × 10¹² M☉, so KiDS's large-radius signal must come from the lens's surroundings, not its own phantom. That points at the linear 2-halo template. H0 and H1 failed. | soft edge worse |
| CFG23 | The ΛCDM control in the same machinery. NFW halos (Duffy+08 c, Tinker+10 bias as the 2-halo cap) run through the exact KiDS fit and FP1's LG integrator, with the edge x_t R200m scanned like the framework's x_e. Duffy-c halos miss KiDS (χ² 173.7 against the framework's 104.7; the misfit sits at R ≥ 0.6 Mpc, the vanilla halo model's transition region), so the pre-declared reading is void. But c × 0.7 reaches **χ² 99.3**, so KiDS does not prefer the framework. And every standard-halo variant has a KiDS–LG tension T = 24.5–72.5 (baseline 55.4) against the framework's 26–28: **CFG21/22's 5–7σ is not framework-specific**; the LG's spherical R₀ is the weak link. The first-written C2 failed (its declared mass range was the timing-argument mass) and stays scored. Diagnostics (CFG23_diagnostics, post-hoc): the NFW projection is exact to 2.7e-4; **against KiDS's own best edge the cold budget's edge costs 40.9–62.0 (6.4–7.9σ)**, so the budget window is open only under CFG4's lenient tolerance. | LG conflict not framework-specific; budget window closed against KiDS's best (template caveat) |
| CFG24 | The cold budget with turned-around associations as the top-level systems (FG001 at T3's own level; CFG12's untested next step). KT2017's groups are linked friends-of-friends in redshift space at the combined system's self-consistent turnaround radius (no new constant; generous to the budget). Associations lower the phantom sum (R(0.35) 0.870 → 0.772; the largest association holds 11% of the baryons) and move the canonical P2 budget edge from 0.354 to 0.390, cutting KiDS's cost against its own best from 38.6 to **29.4** (ν_mono 45.7 → 32.9; alt 45.4–49.3). The most generous linking still leaves 20.8–35.3. H1 (cost ≤ 9) FAILED, as declared. **Erratum for CFG11/12/17 found here:** the D ≤ 15 sample holds the Milky Way's own group at V/75 = 0.12 Mpc with a bogus M_b = 4.4e12 M☉ (47% of the sample's baryons); dropping it moves every FG001 edge up by ~0.006 (canonical P2 0.3483 → 0.3542) and CFG23's V4 costs down by ~2. | budget–KiDS gap narrowed, not closed (5.4σ canonical); MW-entry erratum |
| CFG25 | The ΛCDM control for FG016's infall ("where does the cold fluid go?"). FG016's six collapse runs are re-run bit for bit (table and KiDS numbers reproduced to 0) and scored as a *standard* halo: the collapse's own interior and infall, mass free per KiDS bin. Best KiDS χ² 173.0 (A = 0, s = 2.2) and 149.5 (2-halo capped, s = 1.2), against good fits at 99–105 and FG016's framework profile at 180.6–224.6. The misfit sits at R ≥ 0.6 Mpc, as in CFG23. H1 FAILED, so per the declared reading **the KiDS side of FG016's kill is not framework-specific**: spherical infall around an isolated power-law seed misses KiDS's outer profile for standard halos too. Caveat: the standard halo's χ² falls with accretion rate, and the best sits at the grid's top (s = 2.2). FG016's derived edge (0.18–0.27) stands, but the window it was compared to has a KiDS-machinery lower end. | FG016's KiDS kill not framework-specific; the infall environment is the untested ingredient |
| CFG26 | Aborted before its main run. The infall seeded by the constrained mean profile around a z = 0.25 turnaround region (`ics_constrained`, the peak's own surroundings). The seed is flat inside the region (inner slope 0.054–0.068), so it collapses like a top hat: no halo at z_l (M200m 1–4% of M_ta, splashback 0.04–0.06 r_ta), and the framework profile gives χ² ≈ 350. The underdense variant (t_env = −1) gives NaN. The routine's fix (`eps_in`) reintroduces a scanned choice, so the lane was stopped. The MUTATE log is kept as the record. No result is claimed. | aborted: the seed routine gives no halo without eps_in |
| CFG27 | Closing the edge thread (bookkeeping on committed numbers; the outcome was known when written). Rule: a tension counts as an exclusion only if it exceeds the spread the same KiDS machinery demonstrably gives standard halos under reasonable modelling choices; the smallest such spread is S = 50.2 (CFG25's capped spherical-infall halo against CFG23's best NFW), with 73.7 and 74.4 for the others. The budget-vs-KiDS cost with turned-around associations (CFG24) is 29.4 / 32.9 canonical (0.59 / 0.66 of S) and 45.4 / 49.3 alt (0.90 / 0.98). **The edge thread closes as not decidable with this machinery, not as an exclusion**; the alt footing is at the margin. MUTATE (S = 0) fails H1. Program standing written to STANDING_2026-09-28.md. | edge thread closed: undecidable with present machinery |
| CFG28 | Adversarial referee of candidate B's largest failure, the MW ultra-faints (FG001: 7.97 / 7.52σ). FG001 had dropped the 9 upper-limit systems and quoted statistical errors only. Kaplan–Meier with the limits gives +0.325 / +0.304 dex (from +0.355 / +0.334), and a conservative systematic floor (Υ_V 1–4, estimator) of 0.077 dex brings it to **3.8 / 3.5σ**. Tides do not explain it: beyond 80 kpc +0.325 ± 0.029 against +0.322 inside, ρ = −0.22 (p = 0.23). Noise does not: the best-measured 13 give +0.432 ± 0.056. A constant floor (binaries) fits slightly worse than a mass factor (Δln L −1.7 / −1.5) and would need 2.8 km/s in every system. H1 and H2 passed: **the failure is real but was overstated**. Binaries remain the one escape; the decisive test is multi-epoch dispersions. | UFD failure real, 3.5–3.8σ (not 8σ) |
| CFG29 | The binary audit of the ultra-faint failure. Published bounds used as fixed inputs: single-epoch binaries rarely push dispersions much above ~4.5 km/s (McConnachie & Côté 2010), and a 10-year multi-epoch baseline leaves ≲10–120% bias only below ~1 km/s (Ou et al. 2026). **H1 passed:** where binaries are weakest (predicted ≥ 1.5 km/s, 19 + 2 limits) the offset is +0.313 / +0.293 dex, 3.8 / 3.5σ. **H2 passed:** Eridanus II (6.9 ± 1.1 km/s at 372 kpc, predicted 3.05) and Ursa Major I (7.2 ± 1.1 at 102 kpc, predicted 1.92) exceed the single-epoch ceiling by 2.3–2.4σ. The median binary floor needed is 3.37 km/s, and 26 of 31 need more than 2 km/s. **Binaries do not explain the failure**, which stands at 3.5–3.8σ. | UFD failure survives the binary audit |
| CFG30 | Binary galaxies (B1, the largest unscored risk) refereed under candidate B. h48b's sample, estimator and prior exec'd read-only (2MRS major pairs, d3 > 5 r_p, N = 1830); the one change is the orbit: h48b's circular speeds vs the timing argument's (radial first approach from the Big Bang in each law's two-body force + Λ). Controls: h48b reproduced; the timing solver to 1e-12–3e-4; the Local Group's first approach reproduces FP11's full two-body integration to 0.3–0.4%; **C3 failed marginally** (the estimator reads the timing distribution 5.5% high). **H1 passed:** under circular orbits even the most generous baryons (Υ_K 1.0, +0.3 mag, doubled xGASS gas) leave A = 1.51 / 1.44 (12.9 / 11.7σ). **H2 passed (seen before):** the circular model fails the data's own separation profile for both laws (B 6.5σ, ΛCDM 5.4σ). **H3 passed (headline):** under B's timing orbits A = 1.116 / 1.047 (bias-corrected 1.06 / 0.99). **H4 failed:** the timing profile is too steep (1.04 → 1.39, −4.2σ). **H5 passed:** ΛCDM's halos under timing over-predict (0.46). Reported: mass trend +5.3σ for B (ΛCDM −4.7σ, opposite); the same timing makes MW–M31 1.6–1.75× too fast (FP11 T4). MUTATE (velocities halved) fails H3. The MUTATE run exposed two control bugs (fixed, tolerances unchanged); R0 and R7's ΛCDM columns were added after it. | B1 is orbit-degenerate, not an established failure; binary galaxies do not discriminate B from ΛCDM |
| CFG31 | The Coma UDGs (B2, recorded 4.9 / 4.7σ on the external-field reading) re-scored under candidate B's ownership rule: an accreted system obeys the isolated law of its infall baryons (as CFG18 did for the Local Group's satellites). Not blind: L23's stars-only isolated offset (+0.397 / +0.357 dex) was known. L23's data, estimator and weighting exec'd read-only; infall gas drawn from CFG18's calibration set (42 LVD field dwarfs, 5 nearest in log M_*); the floor recomputed for the isolated law (0.161 dex, M/L 0.078 because deep MOND scales as √M; the external-field entries dropped). Controls: L23 and CFG18 reproduced. **H1 passed:** +0.234 / +0.195 dex = **1.3 / 1.1σ**. **H2 passed:** DF44 alone 0.7 / 0.5σ. **H3 passed:** the UDGs agree with CFG18's M31 dwarfs under the same rule (0.4σ). Reported: stars only 2.3 / 2.1σ; the external-field rival 4.9 / 4.7σ; nulling the offset would need gas/star 4.5 / 3.7. MUTATE (dispersions doubled) fails H1. C1 first failed on a control bug (L23's printed isolated lines use h9's a0); fixed, tolerance unchanged. | B2 passes under B's rule: the 4.9σ belongs to the external-field reading |
| CFG32 | The X-ray ellipticals (B4, Humphrey+2006: seven galaxies, 'need 1.69× more boost', never scored under B and never given a significance) refereed. B's prediction is the law (T5: galaxies are phantom-dominated; no external field). h10's data and mass model exec'd read-only; per-galaxy medians over 5–70 kpc; galaxy-to-galaxy error; floor from the IMF (Kroupa→Salpeter, 0.126) and the radial range (≤ 40 kpc, 0.065). **H1 failed:** +0.280 / +0.254 dex (a factor 1.91 / 1.79) at **1.70 / 1.58σ**, below the 2σ needed to call it established. **H2 passed:** the shortfall grows outward in 7 of 7 (missing extended mass). **H3 passed:** B's cluster max rule applied at each radius over-predicts 10 kpc by 2.4–5.5× in 6 of 7 (not an escape). Carried by NGC 720, 4649 and 4472 (would need 9–15× their stellar mass in hot gas inside 70 kpc); four sit near zero. Salpeter alone: 1.45 / 1.26σ. MUTATE (g_obs halved) fails H1. | B4 is a large shortfall (factor ~1.9) but not established (1.6–1.7σ); decided by the deprojected gas profiles |
| CFG33 | The SLACS Einstein radii (B7, '14–18% short' at Salpeter) as a lensing-vs-dynamics consistency test inside candidate B. B's prediction is the law (T5; no external field). α_lens: the stellar-mass normalisation B's law needs for mean convergence 1 at each lens's Einstein radius (h53's machinery exec'd read-only, ν_mono). α_dyn: the normalisation B's law needs to reproduce ATLAS3D's JAM mass inside r_1/2 (h9's convention), fitted in log σ and evaluated at each lens's dispersion. Floor 0.10 dex declared. Controls: h53 and h9 reproduced. **H1 passed:** κ̄ at Salpeter 0.81–0.86. **H2 passed (headline), narrowly:** α_lens 1.20–1.27× Salpeter vs α_dyn 0.85–0.86×, a gap of +0.15–0.17 dex = **1.5–1.7σ with the floor, 7–8σ statistically**. **H3 failed:** B's dynamics need an IMF rising with σ at only 2.7σ (declared 3σ). MUTATE (Einstein masses doubled) fails H2. | B7 passes only within the cross-survey IMF floor: lensing needs ×1.45 the stellar mass B's dynamics give at the same σ |
| CFG34 | The a₀ ladder (B3: 'the cluster rung 6.3σ from the deep tail', a phantom-only reading) re-scored under candidate B, plus its one untested rung: the 20 Lovisari+2015 X-ray groups under B's max rule as written (M_B = M_b + max(phantom, 5.364 M_b), CFG4's convention), with h7's data and SHMR stars exec'd read-only; floor from the stellar bracket and a 20% hydrostatic bias. Controls: h7's η and CFG4's X-COP identity ratios reproduced. **H1 passed:** at R500 M_HSE/M_B = 1.41 / 1.33 = **1.8 / 1.5σ** (the phantom wins the max in 18–20 of 20; ~10σ on the group-to-group error alone). **H2 failed:** inside R2500 1.88 = **2.6σ** (the cosmic share wins but is short: f_b 0.083). **H3 passed:** the shortfall tracks the baryon fraction over groups and X-COP clusters, ρ = −0.958 (p = 7e-18). The ladder under B: clusters 0.7σ; the only clean standing failure is the UFDs (3.5–3.8σ). MUTATE (HSE masses tripled) fails H1. | B3's 6.3σ does not apply to B; B's cosmic share, tied to today's baryons, is short in baryon-depleted groups (and ellipticals) |
| CFG35 | The cold-matter rule derived from T4 (a pressureless, conserved cold fluid keeps its collapse mass; T5's 'today's baryons' is a floor) in its conservation form: dark = phantom + leftover collapse debris (NFW), with M_coll from the Moster SHMR. **H1b passed:** the X-ray ellipticals close (−0.013 / −0.024 dex, 0.1–0.2σ; the law alone +0.28). **H2 failed:** the same rule breaks the most massive SPARC spirals (f_ex = 0 in 88%; v_c at R_HI raised up to +0.30 dex in log M_* ≈ 11.3–11.5 spirals, e.g. UGC 2885). | CFG36 | CFG35's conservation rule with measured colour-split collapse masses (Mandelbaum+2016 weak lensing, Table 3, fetched with approval; red for ellipticals and S0s, blue for late types). **H1 failed narrowly:** the ellipticals half-close (+0.125 dex, 1.04σ; law +0.28; colour-blind 0.1σ). **H2 failed on one galaxy:** every late-type spiral is now clean (f_ex = 0, incl. UGC 2885), but the S0 UGC 2487 (red) would rotate +0.14 dex too fast. H2 restricted to the table's range (log M_* ≥ 10) after the MUTATE run exposed a clamping artefact (disclosed). MUTATE (colours swapped) fails H2. | The split saves star-forming spirals; passive disks become the test |
| CFG37 | The passive-disk test: den Heijer+2015's 16 ATLAS3D early types with HI circular velocities at 8–28 kpc (Table 1, fetched with approval), under the law and under CFG36's conservation rule (red collapse masses). **H1 passed:** the law fits them, +0.026 ± 0.085 dex (0.3σ). **H2 failed:** at log M_* 10.3–10.9 the red collapse masses are fully consumed by the phantom (f_ex = 0 in all 16), so the rule adds nothing to test. | The law passes on passive disks; the rule bites only above log M_* ≈ 11.2, where data are scarce |
| CFG38 | SLUGGS globular-cluster dispersions of 19 early types (h50's data, bins and Jeans machinery exec'd read-only) under B's derived conservation rule with Mandelbaum's measured red collapse masses. **H1 passed:** the rule +0.007 ± 0.017 (0.4σ) where the law is +0.080 (3.3σ); it adds mass in the 10 galaxies with log M_* ≥ 11.2 (M87 +0.28 → +0.08, NGC 4374 +0.21 → +0.02) and equals the law below. **H2 passed** (weak bite): the mass trend goes (−1.2σ; law +1.8σ). Post-hoc: ΛCDM on the same halos −0.037 (−2.1σ). MUTATE (collapse masses /100) fails H1. | The derived rule fits massive ellipticals without touching spirals or passive disks; never 'data favour the framework' |

Controls: DM reproduces CFG45 to 1e-6, the generic function equals h48's to 1e-9, profiles match numerical integration to 1.6e-11, the absurd profile flips the gate; MUTATE fails the headline; a first-run control bug (monotonicity on h48's clip plateau) fixed and disclosed. | The debris result is robust to concentration and Einasto, not to a core: a cusp closes the ultra-faints and over-predicts the classical dwarfs, a core removes both; the A coefficients need checking |

Needed (not fetched, needs the user's go): Forbes+2017 Tables 2-4, Usher+2012 / Alabi+2016 / Harris+2013 photometry and profiles. Not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG82_gc_slope_feasibility/README.md |

A flat offset is not established either. The decisive test remains multi-epoch dispersions. Not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG83_ufd_luminosity_trend/README.md |

C2 (H-alpha flux vs catalogue, -0.024 dex) and C3 (redshift, +9.8 km/s) pass. At z 0.6-1.1 the same pipeline reaches the regime (53 / 65 of 114 below a0). | CFG93 | Independent re-derivation of CFG51 / CFG66 (multi-epoch, binary-cleaned ultra-faint dispersions; own code, frozen docstring; the sum-rule rows are inferred from the README, not independent). **The numbers reproduce**: Bootes I +0.2199 +/- 0.0889 (2.47 sigma; 2.4613 with CFG51's radius and floor conventions), Tucana II +0.4652 +/- 0.1285 (3.62 sigma), single-epoch +0.3109 / +0.7300; CFG66: Bootes I mixture s_cold 2.000, s_hot 5.089, f_hot 0.525, total-mixture 2.30 sigma, cold alone -0.071 +/- 0.203, bootstrap P about 0.16; Tucana II gradient |g| = 2.00, p = 0.99. CFG51's README states neither its radius (rhalf_sph_physical) nor its floor convention. **Tucana II fails a stricter test the pipeline never ran: of its 12 cleaned stars only 7 are testable multi-epoch stars, and these alone give offset +0.22 +/- 0.14 (1.55 sigma); the cleaning ladder is monotone (7.48 -> 5.30 -> 4.06 -> 3.45 km/s), the signature of residual binary contamination.** Bootes I cleaning is almost powerless (2 of 33 multi-epoch stars flagged; its excess is compatible with law plus binaries, P(sigma_clean >= 3.91) = 0.03-0.45 across binary populations). Simulation at the real cadence: the flagging catches only 9-13% of injected binaries and the cleaned sigma is biased high by +3-5% (sigma = 4) to +13-18% (sigma = 2) (C3b failed, as declared). The floor dominates the error (0.076 vs 0.046). Not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG93_multiepoch_rederivation/README.md |
| CFG110 | Does the KiDS lensing signal at fixed g_bar depend on lens stellar mass within a colour class? (criteria frozen f870b217a before any subset number; the June agentZ memo seen beforehand, disclosed.) One pass of the June estimator keeping per-lens sums (C1 reproduces the June sums to 3e-11); mass halves at each class's median log M_gal; B's law and CFG67's LCDM stacks computed on the identical subsets (C3 exact); joint 14-bin jackknife covariance (null 13.7 vs 14). **No detectable within-class mass dependence: B's mass-independence consistent (22.2/14, p 0.075); colour-split LCDM consistent (25.2/14, p 0.033); power B vs colour-split 8.8 (< 9): non-discriminating between those; the colour-blind LCDM (Moster) rejected at 105/14 (p ~ 4e-16; power vs B 87).** With the colour split: the signal depends on type, not detectably on mass within type -- B gets the mass-independence right and misses the type dependence; colour-blind halos fit the colour split only through mass, closed here; colour-split halos fit both. Photo-z check: the within-class redshift split is consistent with zero (19.2/14, p 0.16). MUTATE (LCDM contrast injected) fails H1 at 3.0 sigma; informative. R2b post hoc (disclosed). | 2f05b5303 |
| CFG111 | Does the SLUGGS deficit survive published per-galaxy GC density slopes? (criteria frozen d36b680bb before any number.) CFG55's JAM-calibrated pipeline with each galaxy's gamma from Alabi+2017's literature relation (gamma = -0.63 log M* + 9.81, clipped 2-4; read from the paper's HTML and verified against its Table 1 to 0.000 using on-disk SLUGGS masses; 2.49-3.43, mean 2.79) instead of the fixed gamma = 3 (CFG76: law zero at 1.83, rule at 2.45). **The gamma caveat does not rescue the law: +0.082 +- 0.023 dex, 3.6 sigma (alt 3.2; was 4.0 at gamma = 3); the gamma that would null the massive centrals is far below their published values (NGC 4365 1.06 vs 2.56, NGC 4374 1.13 vs 2.56, M87 none >= 1). B's derived rule fits with the same slopes: +0.028, 1.55 sigma (alt 1.64; population masses -0.66).** Brackets gamma +-0.4: law 2.3-4.7 sigma, rule -0.4 to 3.3. Without the four centrals: law 2.2 sigma, rule 0.7. Controls exact (C1 0.0, C2 0.000); MUTATE (gamma = 1.83) fails H1 and H2; informative. Isotropy assumed. | 6e1b04092 |
| CFG100 | Independent re-derivation of CFG110 (KiDS within-colour-class stellar-mass test; own code, frozen text first; the per-lens sums come from CFG110's staged product, so independence stops at sum level). **Every headline reproduces**: B 22.20 / 14 (p 0.0746; alt 22.198), colour-split LCDM 25.16 (p 0.033), colour-blind Moster 105.04, R0 = 8.77 (below 9), per-class chi2 to 0.5%. **'B gets mass-independence right' is NOT supported: only a non-rejection is.** Power: the omnibus 14-dof test detects LCDM-size dependence 41% of the time and half-size 11%; the 1-dof amplitude fit gives A-hat_LCDM = 0.33 +/- 0.34 (LCDM size disfavoured at 2.0 sigma, half-size not excluded; the declared W3 fails). B and LCDM are not discriminated: B's p ranges 0.005-0.08 across mass-split edges and the order flips with the split or by dropping bin 9; R0 = 8.8 against 9 is razor-thin (pair weights 10.35). Only the Moster rejection is robust (through the bin set and errors x1.5; not in size to the M* calibration, 49-230). Not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG100_kids_mass_rederivation/README.md |
| CFG101 | Independent re-derivation of CFG50 (the tidal-tensor closure of Gap 2; own sympy and numerics, frozen docstrings with sha256; CFG50's script bodies opened only after the frozen runs). **The CFG50 headline reproduces** (reaction/g_law 7.3 -> 1.318, 3.1 -> 0.541, 0.23 -> 0.034 from a calibration on the 0.3 h values; ghost boundary x91.49; chi range x63.8; reaction opposite to the fluid force inside r < 1.8 h, not 1.7 h) **with one real difference: the ghost-free ceiling is 0.5008 g_tot, not 0.505** (CFG50's D2 grid starts at r0 = 0.01 h, 0.75% too high). Reciprocity is a translation-invariance consequence (a 3-D Noether check with non-spherical baryons and anisotropic Pi to 6e-11), not a spherical artefact. **Load-bearing: the coupling form (the 0.5 belongs to the tidal line only; freeing h_ij under ghost-freedom alone makes the force unbounded, and with |h| <= 1 the reaction per unit support falls 17-19x but not to zero, and only the 1e12 galaxy reaches CFG44's 0.10 g_law) and the definition of the fluid-side force (CFG50 omits the h_ij v^j momentum terms; the exact geodesic balance flips the radial term).** A non-linear coupling does not lift the ceiling at r = h. Open: non-perturbative response at |h| about 1, a non-spherical target, FRW for non-tidal couplings, a prescribed non-dynamical source (ownership as initial data). Scoped confirmation of the no-go, not a proof of closure. Not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG101_tidal_closure_rederivation/README.md |
| CFG113 | Does GC orbital anisotropy rescue the law's SLUGGS deficit? (criteria frozen 8ef01b411 before any number; the orchestrator approved the lane.) CFG111's JAM-calibrated pipeline with published per-galaxy GC slopes and a constant GC anisotropy beta in a declared bracket, -0.5 to +0.5, spanning the measured early-type GC systems (NGC 5846 red ~0.4 / blue ~0.15 outside 3 R_e; NGC 1407 and M87 mixed signs; read from arXiv abstracts). **Anisotropy inside the measured range does not rescue the law: the deficit never falls below +0.074 +- 0.022 dex, 3.4 sigma (alt 2.9), at beta = +0.5; no beta < 1 nulls its mean (+0.041 at 0.99 on an extended grid); only beta ~ 0.9 on the alt footing reaches 1.9 sigma.** The rule fits for isotropic or radial orbits (0.56 sigma at +0.5) and goes just past 2 sigma for tangential ones (2.06 at -0.5). At gamma = 3 beta barely matters (law 3.9-4.2 sigma). C1 beta = 0 reproduces CFG111's 64 offsets exactly; C2 the Jeans solver matches the analytic flat-v_c result to 2e-4. MUTATE (the mean deficit removed, scatter kept) fails H1 at 0.00 sigma, rc 1; informative. R7 (post hoc, disclosed) bounds the solver's grid-edge bias near beta = 1 (<= 0.0025 dex). Caveats: constant beta only; one beta for red and blue GCs. | 1db2d3a69 |
| CFG112 | With published per-galaxy GC slopes, does one debris fraction fit all ten populations at 2 sigma? (criteria frozen 663f73877 before any number; asked because CFG111 restored the rule's SLUGGS fit.) CFG71's harness with SLUGGS's gamma_i from CFG111; the other nine populations from CFG71's committed scan; CFG75's grid-point intersection. **NO, narrowly: SLUGGS (dynamical masses) fits only for phi in [0.77, 1] against the other nine's [0.23, 0.71] (alt [0.21, 0.66]); the M31 LVD binds; gap 0.06 in phi (alt 0.11).** It opens with every gamma_i lowered by 0.2 ([0.37, 0.71]) or with SLUGGS's population masses; stays empty with +0.2; at 3 sigma all ten share [0.31, 1] (alt [0.16, 1]); 1 sigma empty. The STANDING clause 'and not with dynamical SLUGGS masses' stands. Controls exact: C1 (CFG71's scan at gamma = 3, 101 points), C2 (CFG111's law and rule), C3 (CFG75's intersection without SLUGGS). MUTATE (gamma = 3) reproduces CFG75's empty intersection and its 3-sigma [0.77, 1]; uninformative for H1 (both runs fail it; declared in advance). Radial GC orbits (CFG113) could open the gap; not computed. | 670356510 |
| CFG114 | Does the SLUGGS deficit survive GC slopes and orbits varied together? (criteria frozen c5b32b499 before any number.) The 5 x 5 box of CFG111's slope shifts (every gamma_i + delta, delta -0.4..+0.4) and CFG113's constant GC anisotropy bracket (beta -0.5..+0.5), CFG55's JAM-calibrated masses. **The law's deficit exceeds 2 sigma in 23 of 25 cells (alt 21) but not in the corner where shallower slopes and radial orbits combine: at (gamma_i - 0.4, beta = +0.5) it is +0.013 +- 0.022 dex, 0.6 sigma (alt 0.1; population masses -0.24; no centrals -0.58).** The corner puts every slope 0.4 below the published relation (mean 2.39) and every GC on orbits more radial than any measured system. On the alt footing the slope shift -0.4 alone gives 1.86 sigma. The rule is conditional in the opposite corner: it fits in 13 of 25 cells and fails wherever the slopes are 0.2+ steeper (2.06-3.47 sigma), at beta = -0.5 (2.06) and at the law's corner (-2.59). So SLUGGS cannot make either reading clean; measured GC slopes and anisotropy for the massive centrals would decide it. C1 reproduces CFG111 and CFG113 exactly (40 comparisons). MUTATE (deficit removed at the weakest cell) fails H1 as required, but so does the main run: uninformative for H1 (declared in advance). | 2a83f369e |
| CFG104 | Independent re-derivation of CFG112 (universal debris fraction phi at 2 sigma with published SLUGGS GC slopes; own phi layer on the CFG76 estimator; the other nine populations are read from CFG71's committed JSON, not re-derived). **CFG112's headline reproduces exactly at the 0.01 grid on both footings**: SLUGGS window [0.77, 1], the other nine [0.23, 0.71] (alt [0.21, 0.66]), M31 LVD binding (the ultra-faints set the lower edge), ten-population 2 sigma intersection empty, 3 sigma [0.31, 1]; gamma = 3 MUTATE fails the declared checks. **It is a boundary, not a conflict: the ten populations overlap at 2.07 sigma canonical (2.09 alt) against the 2 sigma line (2.62 at gamma = 3); only SLUGGS and the LVD are in tension.** A uniform slope shift of -0.1 opens the ten-set, +0.1 empties SLUGGS's own window: a knife edge in a slope uncertain by more than 0.1; with Gaussian slope scatter 0.2 the ten-set is non-empty in 35-44% of draws. **The 'published slopes' are one relation gamma = clip(-0.63 log M* + 9.81, 2, 4) (2.49-3.43), not per-galaxy values; if projected rather than 3-D, SLUGGS sits at +5.6 sigma at phi = 1.** Not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG104_universal_phi_rederivation/README.md |
| CFG103 | Independent re-derivation of CFG43 (the a0-Lambda tie on a conserved fluid's stress cap; own sympy Euler-Lagrange, own Dirac code, own growth solver; CFG43's script bodies read only after the frozen runs). **The algebra and the obstruction reproduce**: A1 no new local dof (Dirac count D = 2N + 2 with 2N - 1 first-class, no tertiary constraint; new: the only propagating root is the sound mode with c_s^2 = n rho_nn/rho_n); A2 flat a0, Lambda_eff constant to 4e-10, dust limit; A3 pressure P_cap x^2/(1+x^2), the tie, nu_min(5%) within 1-3% of the referee, exponent 1.501, g0 = 0.363 (5%) and 1.07 (20%) with CFG43's setup (the frozen primary 0.205 differed by a mass-ratio convention: the agent misread f_h as a size factor, corrected post hoc). **The 11x ceiling is confirmed as convention-dependent: 11.3 under F = 1/2, d <= 50%, k in {pi, 2pi}/R; 353 with k = 1/R; 1.4e4-1.1e5 only with F >= 0.01 (the cap carrying 1% of the pressure); 0 of 480 grid points reach 1e4 with F >= 0.9 (best case 134 with a sharp cap); the hydrostatic target needs P/P_cap >= 1 inside r_M, a stronger obstruction.** g0 is kappa-independent; the arctan shape is not load-bearing beyond a factor of about 2; the barotropic EOS is load-bearing (forced by the action) and c_s = 0 fluids are untouched. Open: nonlinear collapse inside overdensities (the most plausible loophole), a non-barotropic cap. One frozen check failed (a4, Omega_Lambda 0.685 vs 0.6847). Six MUTATE runs fail as required; agent bugs fixed and disclosed. Not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG103_fluid_tie_rederivation/README.md |
| CFG105 | Independent re-derivation of CFG113 (does GC orbital anisotropy rescue the law's SLUGGS deficit?; an independently written Jeans/anisotropy solver on the CFG76 estimator with the Alabi-relation slopes; CFG113's script not opened before the runs). **The constant-beta bracket reproduces to 0.006 sigma**: the law's minimum 3.38 sigma (alt 2.93) at beta = +0.5; the rule 0.56 sigma at +0.5 and 2.06 at -0.5; H1 holds; the only difference is beta = 0.9 (0.036 sigma, CFG113's own 3e4 kpc grid-edge bias). Solver: the Jeans equation solved on demand by Gauss-Legendre with the integrating factor, no grid edge, Binney-Mamon LOS projection; controls: isothermal and power-law closed forms to 5e-6 or better, Jeans residual 4.3e-6, MUTATE 2 (dropping the projection factor) fails the solver controls. **Attack: radially varying beta does not carry the constant-beta bracket over: Osipkov-Merritt with the tracer extended to infinity removes the law's deficit (r_a = 3 R_e: -0.8 sigma), but the effect comes from the infinite extrapolation (beta -> 1 with gamma near 2.5 makes sigma_r^2 about 5x isotropic): with the tracer cut off at 50 or 20 R_e radial orbits worsen the deficit (beta = +0.5: 3.97 and 4.98 sigma), so the shrinking at large beta is not measured.** The outer tracer slope matters as much as beta (flatter outward: law 1.1 sigma at beta = +0.5), a projected-instead-of-3D slope reading moves the law to 6-8 sigma, and the rule's 2.06 sigma at beta = -0.5 is fragile to leave-one-out. Not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG105_anisotropy_rederivation/README.md |
| CFG106 | Independent re-derivation of CFG114 (the 5 x 5 GC-slope x anisotropy box; new constant-beta Jeans solver on the CFG76 estimator; a reproduction against printed targets, not blind). **CFG114's headline reproduces cell for cell**: the law above 2 sigma in 23/25 (canonical) and 21/25 (alt) cells, the rule fitting in 13/25 on each footing, the corner (-0.4, +0.5) law +0.0126 +/- 0.0214 (0.586 sigma; alt 0.076) and rule -2.591 sigma; all 100 cell means and errors within 1.7e-4 dex (CFG114's h50 Jeans code truncates the LOS integral at u = 6, a 0.57% sigma_pred bias at the corner). MUTATE fails G3-G5. **Attack: the slopes are one relation (Alabi, coefficients -0.63 +/- 0.17 and 9.81 +/- 1.94, correlation not on disk) shifted coherently, so the 2 sigma failure is only as firm as the unknown correlation (fraction with law z < 2: 4.6% at rho = -0.99, 43-47% at rho = 0); per-galaxy scatter does not help; the corner is a mean cancellation (6 galaxies still under-predicted by > 0.05 dex, four over-predicted, M87 would need gamma about 1.6, below the relation's 2-4 range), a two-parameter fit on the box boundary; an error inflation of 20% takes 23/25 to about 21/25; a calibration convention change gives 17/25 to 21/25.** A statement about a box edge; the rule over-predicts at the same corner (-2.59 sigma). Not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG106_slope_beta_box_rederivation/README.md |
| CFG97 | Independent re-derivation of CFG41 (Di Teodoro+2023 massive spirals) and its selection-bias Monte Carlo (own code, frozen docstrings and script hashes; CFG36's machinery forbidden, so the rule side could not be reconstructed). **The law side reproduces to three decimals**: 15 corrected -0.028 +/- 0.066 (-0.43 sigma; alt -0.044), S0/S0a +0.003 +/- 0.071 (+0.05 sigma), without the two flagged galaxies -0.038, all error components identical; **the Monte Carlo reproduces almost exactly** (MATCH biases 0.0667 and 0.0845, range 0.066-0.085, grid 0.003-0.154); **the rule's -0.105 +/- 0.131 is NOT reproduced** (a consistency test shows f_ex is not a simple velocity-squared fraction). CFG53's law-side shape numbers reproduce only with inverse-variance pooling scaled by sqrt(chi2/dof). **New: B is not additive (recovered-minus-injected error +0.018 / -0.018 / -0.035 at delta = -0.05 / +0.05 / +0.10, B shrinking by about 0.35 x delta), so the S0 corrected offset moves from +0.003 to about +0.019 (+0.27 sigma); B is not fixed by the data (B = 0 / 0.058 / 0.076 / 0.081 gives S0 +0.079 / +0.021 / +0.003 / -0.002); the S0 sign flips under leave-one-out; the P2 kernel moves the law offset by about 0.023 dex.** Failed controls kept (C2 10/15 within 3 km/s as in CFG41; C3a numerical fault repaired post hoc; C5 via M3). MUTATE fails H1 and H2 as required. Not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG97_massive_spirals_rederivation/README.md |
| CFG115 | Is the KiDS colour dependence at fixed g_bar a step at the red/blue valley or a gradient? (criteria frozen e416f3bea before any colour-binned lensing number; orchestrator-approved.) Six within-class colour-tertile bins from the June lens stage's own LePhare u-r (recovered by exact RA/Dec match for all 181,477 lenses; u-r > 2.0 reproduces every June label), common-weight amplitudes over K1, leave-one-patch-out jackknife. **By the frozen map NON-DISCRIMINATING (power 4.8 < 9). The data: no sharp step -- the tertiles either side of 2.0 are equal (-0.049 each); a within-class-constant step is rejected (18.1/4, p 0.001); a linear colour gradient fits (6.3/4, +0.34 +- 0.06 dex/mag); the within-class variation sits in the boundary tertiles (outer contrasts p 0.20), as a colour-noise-blurred step would give.** B's null 36.3/5 with a free offset. Descriptive models (no colour noise): colour-split LCDM's sharp class step 21.1/5, colour-blind Moster 12.2/5. R6 (post hoc, disclosed): the frozen amplitude definition leaves a near-null normalisation direction (condition 3200) that inflates zero-offset rows (frozen R1 305/6, R5 304/77.6/29.2); a fixed reference (condition 10.8) reproduces every fitted chi2 exactly. C1-C3 pass (null mean 3.76/4); MUTATE recovers an injected within-class slope (0.298 vs 0.270) but is uninformative for H1 (both fail; declared). | e469ab0b1 |
| CFG107 | Independent re-derivation of CFG115 (KiDS within-class colour tertiles; independent code on the calc chat's staged per-lens sums, checked only against the June file to 3e-11, so independence stops at sum level; definitions read from CFG115's frozen text). **Reproduces**: amplitudes -0.1652 -0.2669 -0.0491 -0.0487 +0.0501 +0.1226; step chi2 18.108/4 (p 0.0012); gradient 6.290/4 with slope 0.337 +/- 0.061 dex/mag; B's null 36.27/5; condition numbers 3198 vs 10.78; 37 lines reproduced, 2 approximate, 1 not reproduced with a found cause (the colour-split LCDM zero-offset jackknifed-reference row 77.6 uses a class-mix reference: 22.7 vs 77.7, a normalisation artefact against a near-null covariance direction). Power 4.8 needs the class-level-amplitude reading (4.465 with the step-fit reading); both below 9. **A wording error in CFG115's Disclosures: the 'fixed reference zero-offset' values 36.2 / 21.1 / 12.2 are the free-offset values (fixed-reference zero-offset: B 38.8).** **Attack: the step rejection is fragile (p 0.05 at x1.38 error inflation; the gradient-vs-step delta chi2 loses 3.84 at x1.82); a blur of at least 0.2 mag in the observed colour fits a step as well as the linear gradient (7.4 vs 6.3); the gradient survives mass/z matching (slope 0.46 +/- 0.11) and is not confined to large R, but signal vs a colour-dependent systematic (IA, satellites, photo-z by colour, M/L) is undecidable with the staged product (mimicking the gradient by M/L alone needs about 0.9 dex).** K4a (BFGS vs normal equations, 1.95e-6 vs 1e-8) failed as frozen (optimiser limit). Not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG107_kids_colour_tertiles_rederivation/README.md |
| CFG116 | Does B's one specific failure (the KiDS early/late split at fixed g_bar) pass the standard lensing systematics nulls? (criteria frozen e4083bd4f before any staging.) | CFG108 | Independent re-derivation of CFG116 (KiDS B-mode / cross-shear null and the near/far source split; independent code on the calc chat's staged per-lens sums, checked against the June file to 3.1e-11 and cfg110_perlens to 1e-10; the near/far assignment and the WX content inside the staging cannot be re-checked). **Every headline reproduces to the displayed digits**: cross difference 5.27/7 (p 0.627), near-far 5.37/7, far-only split 23.15/7 (p 0.0016), near-only 18.82, all-source 35.04, power lambdas 8.98 / 2.25 and 3.04 / 0.76 (CFG116's 'expected chi2' is the non-centrality lambda; the expected chi2 is 7 + lambda), dilution 0.13 / 0.09 dex (the amplitude reproduction needs CFG116's pooled-ESD-ratio definition). **Both nulls have low power against a systematic carrying half the split: cross-null power 0.32 (near/far 0.07) at alpha = 0.01; 80% needs eps about 0.74 (omnibus) or 0.57 (shape-matched); the near/far null is essentially blind to near-only systematics (A_nf = +0.03 +/- 0.29). chi2_7 tails are anti-conservative (permuted 99th percentile about 20.5): the far-only split's empirical p is 0.0055, not 0.0016 (subsamples of the all-source split give P(>= 23.2) = 0.39, so it is not merely a smaller-N fluctuation, but the splits are dependent).** 'Passes both nulls' does not exclude E-mode-only class-dependent errors (shear calibration would need 51%; a baryonic-mass or g_bar offset about 0.5 dex, the largest untested avenue), lens photo-z (a 0.24 offset needed), satellites (isolation flags unread), or covariance x1.8 (all-source p 0.05 at x1.58; the nulls lose their power too). Not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG108_kids_nulls_rederivation/README.md |
| CFG140 | a0 at z ~ 1.5 from KURVS-CDFS (Puglisi+2023): the 10 rotation-supported discs' outermost MEASURED rotation points (R_max 9-13 kpc, 4-7 R_d), flat a0 vs a0 ~ H(z) (E = 2.4), one pipeline with a SPARC z = 0 same-pipeline anchor (75 galaxies) and a KROSS z ~ 0.85 control (390); gas unmeasured -> bracket mu 0.25-4; M* +-0.2 dex; pressure support P0 (none) and P1 (Burkert constant sigma) carried everywhere (criteria 28dca75b8; pre-run C2 correction 49baf209f). **NON-DIAGNOSTIC over the brackets; the answer turns on the outer pressure support:** P0 (a strict lower bound on V_c): a0 ~ H(z) over-predicts in all 24 cells (-3.2 to -9.6 sigma) and flat a0 is never disfavoured; P1: flat a0 under-predicts in 21/24 and the rival is never disfavoured. The sample is in the decisive regime (g_bar/a0 0.06-0.67; 3.1-5.1 sigma statistical separation per cell). The KROSS differential under P1 (+0.27 +- 0.07) exceeds even the rival's +0.08, a caution that P1 over-corrects at KURVS radii. Controls pass (anchor median +0.064 dex); MUTATE (v x 2) fails H1 in 48/48, informative. Settles with: an outer dispersion profile or a pressure-free tracer, and measured gas. | 5820c92e3 |
| CFG140-corr | Correction to the CFG140 row (logic). V_obs is a lower bound on V_c, so it can exclude only a model predicting V_c < V_obs. Under P0 both readings predict at or above the observed accelerations in every cell (largest P0 Delta' -0.035 flat, -0.196 rival), so the bound excludes neither. 'a0 ~ H(z) over-predicts in all 24 cells' is the NO-PRESSURE-SUPPORT SCENARIO only (the rival needs substantial outer pressure support). 'Flat a0 under-predicts in 21/24' is the constant-sigma Burkert scenario only (flat needs support well below it). | CFG232 | DOOR 13: BIMOND, Milgrom's bimetric MOND (from memory, unverified: Milgrom 2009, PRD 80, 123536), scoped to what the earlier bimetric work (BIMETRIC_PRICE, the WF2 derivative-bimetric gate report of 2026-09-09, NEGATIVE_RESULTS_CLOSURE, the RETRACTIONS ledger) did not score; criteria 79974a5a committed first in b31f5e705 with a prior-work-inherited section (the published BIMOND preprint is not inherited as a survivor and the four withdrawn RETRACTIONS items on this door are not inherited); estimates NOT blind, since the class was chosen after reading WF2/L70. Sub-variants: 13a (Lambda-hat = 0), 13b-i (a second-metric cosmological constant sets a0, Lambda-hat = Lambda_g by hand), 13b-ii (no Lambda in the action, the interaction's constant piece is the vacuum energy), 13c (conformally coupled AQUAL-type scalar, a reproduction control of the single-metric pincer). **A scoped no-go; nothing passes G1 as a derived mechanism.** G1-law passes for all four only as M1-inv (the interaction function solved for from the target by inversion, hand formula H4 verified symbolically); against CFG44's C(r) target it FAILS on the exponential sphere, 0.50 (P2) and 0.54 (nu_mono) at M_b = 1e9 (the frozen claim that nu(g_N) g_N is equivalent to C(r) for P2 was wrong for extended profiles). G1-C (cold-component budget, the author's declared addition): cold in g gives g_tot/g_target = nu(g_target) and the 10% line is crossed at x = 0.483, cold in g-hat reverses the force. G2 UNDEFINED (no real FRW background: T4 - T1 = +3B^2, Q < 0, no B = 0 branch with matter in g only), a growth estimate under a declared continuation FAILS; G3 reaction PASS* and energy FAIL (interaction energy negative, 19-254x the orbital energy; relative-sector excess 1.2-2.8x); G4 strict FAIL; G5a ghost FAIL for 13a and 13b (a reproduction with fresh code of the record's WF2/L70 closure, plus the FRW background), PASS on the conditions checked for 13c; G5b Q2 FAIL (Sun at Saturn 6e3-1.6e4x the bound; MW tide 3e-5 to 5e-5x passes only under ownership), gamma - 1 about -5e-7 PASS; G5c c_T UNDEFINED for BIMOND (the relative TT kinetic term vanishes at the MOND-tuned value), PASS analytic for 13c; G7 UNDEFINED (R-static PASS, R-add FAIL), PASS analytic for 13c. **Does a Lambda-sourced second metric supply the a0-Lambda tie? NO, in neither reading:** 13b-i writes a0 = kappa c^2 sqrt(Lambda-hat/8 pi) in by rule plus Lambda-hat = Lambda_g by hand, and the bimetric structure forces neither; 13b-ii derives kappa^2 = -16 pi beta/(sigma_s M0) in the author's normalisation (kappa = 1/2 needs sigma_s M0 = -64 pi, which matches the record's quotation of eq. 87 and is not evidence about Milgrom's own normalisation), inverted (a0 primary, kappa traded for M0), requiring gamma = beta and a vacuum branch that has no solution with matter in g only. **Findings not in the record:** MOND in this reduction needs M'(0) tuned to a kinetic degeneracy; the record's M proportional to Q^(3/2) gives Newton at low gradient and no r^-1 regime (C10); the relative TT kinetic term vanishes exactly at that tuning; on FRW the interaction argument flips sign relative to the static one; the record's twin sum rule holds only at fixed interaction argument. **Kept failures and wrong expectations:** C6 as frozen FAILED (the 4.0-5.7x figure is not what the CFG7 H1 recipe gives; A6 exits 1 by the convention); C7 partly wrong; the C(r)-equivalence claim, the G5c estimate and the frozen G2 hand numbers (k_Q = 12 with opposite sign, not 3) were wrong; an action-versus-static sign erratum in the frozen text; the constraints-only FRW solver did not converge so no verdict uses it. MUTATE: 12 of 13 run modes bite; A2 MUTATE=M3 does not bite (the ghost counting is independent of gamma/beta), a declared control failure. Literature claims (the exact scalar(s) Upsilon, the sign, the eq. 87 factor being the least certain) are all from memory and unverified. Not tested: the real FRW embedding beyond the declared continuation, non-spherical systems, any scalar-tensor limit other than 13c. In-place re-run by the orchestrator: identical apart from timing. but RC41's own gas column rises +0.285 dex per unit z while the repo's PHIBSS measured molecular gas rises +0.09 +- 0.12, a tilt of -0.19 +- 0.14 (1.4 sigma), and at that tilt the class is W-none (neither law disfavoured beyond about 2 sigma); frozen gas-grid rule WEAKENED for the primary gas model (6 of 9 cells hold 3 sigma), FAILS for the surrogate (5 of 9); frozen mock rule: the rival-over-flat likelihood ratio hits 0.54-0.57 in one plausible-band cell (c = 1, t = -0.15) on the committed CSV, a FAIL as a discriminating statement (0.30-0.31 on the corrected CSV, a borderline pass); with the priors declared before running the honest total tension is 1.9 sigma, but that is prior-driven, not a measurement. **CFG217 post-freeze item:** the referee agrees on direction and size (a baryon-mass tilt of about 0.20-0.25 dex over z 0.6-2.5 makes the rival consistent; 0.33 dex reaches the rival's expected slope exactly; a rival-truth mock with a 0.26 dex tilt reproduces both slopes, mean -0.027 and -0.087 against the observed -0.029 and -0.092); the referee's ORIGINAL statement that flat is disfavoured at 3.6 sigma only near 0.35 dex is WITHDRAWN: the 0.1 dex gap to CFG217 was the referee's own row exclusion (rows whose re-decomposed f_DM leaves (0, 1), 10 low-z baryon-dominated discs that carry the slope); the post-hoc script (CFG233_posthoc_tilt_axis.py, on the calc chat's axis log10(1+z), total change over z 0.6 to 2.5, no exclusion) reproduces the calc chat's flat-versus-flat tensions 0.57 / 1.58 / 2.61 / 3.63 / 4.56 / 5.49 sigma at -0.10 / -0.15 / -0.20 / -0.25 / -0.30 / -0.35 dex to within 0.03 sigma (corrected CSV 0.51 / 1.47 / 2.45 / 3.45 / 4.36 / 5.31), with zero-tilt slopes flat -0.0293 (0.0185) and rival -0.0916 (0.0188) (bootstrap sd of the observed sample, 10,000 resamples, seed 233); flat reaches 3.6 sigma at -0.242 (committed) / -0.252 (corrected) on the log axis without exclusion, -0.231 / -0.244 on the linear axis, and -0.341 / -0.354 with exclusion on the log axis; about 0.10 dex of the gap is the row exclusion and about 0.01 dex the axis. Gas alone reaches the needed tilt only with zero true gas at every z; the tilt the measured-gas comparison points to is about 0.17-0.20 dex at 1.4 sigma, where flat sits at roughly 1.6-2.6 sigma by that table. **Corrected table:** alignment unambiguous (same idx, z, R_e and sigma_0 in all 100 rows) but the corrected values rest on one reader's by-eye transcription; the primary statistic uses only z, R_e, V_c and f_DM, so only rows 36 (f_DM), 43 and 44 (V_c) change it (flat -0.0304, rival -0.0915, 5.27 sigma against the rival); the log M_bar and name rows do not enter; rows 31, 32 and 34 become RC41 members (38/62 to 41/59); variant (d) goes from W-flat to W-mixed; the scatter between f_DM-derived and M_bar-derived g_bar drops from 0.192 to 0.107 dex (f_DM is essentially M_bar plus geometry); no conclusion about the rival changes. **Measured versus model columns:** z and R_e are measured; M_bar, f_DM and V_c(R_e) are model outputs (V_c carries the authors' pressure correction, the P0-versus-P4 pitfall; the relation V_rot^2 = V_circ^2 - 3.36 sigma_0^2 (r/R_e) confirmed by a quote in the data chat's provenance note and by Price+2021); the gas prior is the Tacconi+2020 scaling relation, width unstated in RC100 (0.2 dex in Price+2021); table entries are three-method averages, not one uniform method; the referee's gas model is a proxy from RC41's own gas column. Algebra: at fixed V_c, g_bar -> k g_bar shifts delta by -(1 - s) log k; at fixed V_bar, V_c^2 -> p V_c^2 shifts delta by log p exactly; the test therefore cannot tell a z-dependent gas, pressure or inclination input from a z-dependent a0. **Controls:** M1 (injected slope), M3 (swap laws), M4 (remove RC41), M5 (mirror z) bite; M2 (shuffle z) does not bite (the rival's a0(z) term is mechanical and survives the shuffle); M6 (inject a baryon tilt) does not bite (wrong sign in the frozen prediction; magnitude within 26%); M7 (mixed pairing) does not bite (slope moves 0.001); identical on both tables; kept, with the referee's wrong hand estimates R14-R18, a nu_mono agreement of 3.3e-9 against the frozen 1e-9 line (main's exit check used 1e-8, laxer than frozen), and a disclosure that after its runs were saved the referee opened CFG216's INPUT_CORRECTION_2026-09-29.md (a table with CFG217 rows) without using it to tune anything. In-place re-run by the orchestrator, both tables and the post-hoc script: identical apart from timing. A lean is not a detection; not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG233_rc100_referee/README.md |

no data fetched). **The numbers REPRODUCE (145 of 146 scored lines pass; joins exact 99/318/106/137 and ACE 15; all nine bin tables and drifts; all 27 slope lines; the ACE offsets -0.67 / -0.52 / -0.21 and K values; the peers' JSON matches the referee's to 1e-9 in 215 of 241 numbers, the other 26 being bootstrap-SD noise below 0.003 (19) or definitions (7)), but several framings do not survive.** PARTIAL H-B7: the Stripe82 slope is -0.49, the README's +-0.33 is the analytic SE and the referee's bootstrap SD is 0.376; the two ad slopes need the peer's fallback to the opt-table L_IR for 13 rows (+0.048 master-only against +0.056); control C8 FAILS (dust detections 17 from the 20926 flags and 18 from the 21040 flags; the 'both detected' set of 15 is the same either way); no README typo. **Framing:** 'at the same metallicity': all 15 ACE galaxies lie below the lowest Stripe82 value; '0.03 to 0.10 dex' is the error of a bin mean while the per-galaxy catalogue error is 0.083 to 0.111; 'agree to 0.04' compares CO (H2 + He) with dust-based total gas (H2 + HI). **(a) z slopes at fixed L_IR:** the ad set has one overlap bin (12.0-12.5: 10 local, 17 at z >= 1.6) and the other seven cases have none, so the fixed-luminosity comparison is an extrapolation of a linear L_IR term; within-bin permutation nulls are not centred on zero (-0.070 to +0.032), a functional-form systematic of 0.5 to 1 SE; with sample flags as fixed effects (VIF about 20) 3 of 9 slopes reach >= 2.9 sigma (dax alpha_CO +0.69, xa alpha_CO +0.50, xa X_CI +0.46), a disagreement with 'consistent with zero in every factor' by the referee's frozen rule; a 0.10 dex SMG offset barely moves the with-L_IR slope (<= +0.031) but moves the without-L_IR slope by +0.10 to +0.13; power: 80% for |b| >= 0.145 (ad alpha_CO) to 0.23 (others), a real 0.05 dex change seen with probability 0.21-0.52 across the nine. **(b) Circularity:** catalogue masses are exactly factor x luminosity; the informative content is the raw L_850/L'CO ratio, +0.072 +- 0.081 against the README's +-0.050 on alpha_CO; per-galaxy e_logMH2 0.083-0.111; the z slope of one galaxy set differs by 0.1-0.2 between the three routes. **(c) ACE:** the offset is 1.09 dex wide across 30 prescription cells; alpha_CO in the file is 1.36 x Accurso; ACE's dust opacity is 0.35 dex below Dunne's; ACE's L_850/L'CO ratio is within about -0.09 of the local one only after excluding five local rows with non-positive log L'CO (post hoc; the literal frozen rule gives -0.52); a constant-MW alpha_CO moves the ACE offset from -0.52 to -0.087; verdict: a prescription bracket on an extrapolation, not a z-evolution. **(d) Common-mode bias:** cannot be bounded from anything on disk that is independent of the dynamics under test (Stripe82's dynamical inequality is invalid, aperture mismatch with 68% violating; Amvrosiadis N = 12 gives only a one-sided, dynamics-dependent statement; the Stripe82 HI check flips the offset sign, +0.42). **(e) Three-cornered hat at small N:** sigma_CI^2 < 0 in 82% of bootstrap resamples; sigma_dust 95% interval [-0.03, 0.15]; the 95% interval of the CO-dust mean is +-0.167 and N of about 31 is needed for +-0.05; changing the assumed dust frequency by +-10% moves CO-dust by -0.15 / +0.14; like-for-like CO-dust +0.052 +- 0.063, the two [CI]-dust sources differing by 2.1 sigma. **Controls:** MUTATE M1-M7 bite; M8 does not (the frozen prediction was wrong; kept). Wrong estimates kept: H-A5, H-C1 (one overlap bin, not two), H-C3 mean (9 of 9), H-C6 (9 of 9), H-C10b, H-C16 literal, H-C19b, H-C23c (0.94 against 0.95). Provenance: the repo holds a provenance note only for RC100 and the Dunne TSV is outside the repo with only its sha256 in the manifest; none invented. In-place re-run by the orchestrator, including the post-run compare script: identical apart from timing. A lean is not a detection; not evidence for or against the framework. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG238_gas_calibration_referee/README.md |
| CFG255 | Does the KiDS-1000 lensing signal at fixed g_bar change with lens redshift as a₀ ∝ H(z) predicts? (the orchestrator's lane; criteria 8c925db21 before any script; stage A 2fa69fcd1 committed before stage B; CFG110's R4 and the prior lane b3be606a0 disclosed). Extreme photo-z thirds (median z 0.21 vs 0.39): the rival's predicted change is +0.018 dex against σ_A 0.038, power Δχ²_pred 0.14 / 0.17 (needs 9): **NOT POSSIBLE**. Measured χ²/14 for zero / FLAT / RIVAL / ΛCDM: 19.8 / 19.8 / 19.2 / 15.1, all p > 0.1. **MUTATE fails (FLAT p = 0.10 with the rival injected): NON-DISCRIMINATING.** A stellar-mass drift δ mimics an a₀ drift by δ/2 in the deep regime, so the test needs a z ≈ 1 sample with M* stable to < 0.05 dex; none is public (phase 1a 63fea6817). | NOT POSSIBLE / NON-DISCRIMINATING |
| CFG239 | REFEREE of the DR4-READY-1 seed-noise mechanism on which Amendment 18 (draft rev 2, e90ff1ac4, NOT FILED; later revisions by its owner) rests; criteria 39359607 committed first in 8f549fbd8; questions 21aeb4e01 / 3829f98f3 / 62b165001, answers a51d6f8f2 / 2f2354b18; the builder read directly, the frozen prereg, hash files, builder and the calc chat's files never touched, a scratch mirror never committed; DR3 numbers are CODE-PATH TESTS ONLY, never results (Amendment 7(e)); not blind to the answer numbers seen in PLAN.md and commit messages (declared); no data fetched. **(1) CONFIRMED as stated:** a stamp-returning dummy generator shows 1,272 calls = 6 x 212 in exactly the order and shapes read from the source (one default_rng at line 448, six array-shaped draws per trial, the stage-G call at line 612 passing no seed); on the full array (10,230 pairs) removal at three positions, reversal and permutation leave 0.000% of the other pairs bit-identical; measured median change in sigma_vt 5.0-5.15% against 4.65% predicted and 90th percentile 13.2-13.5% against 11.3% (heavier-tailed per-pair scatter); removal is indistinguishable from a different-seed re-draw (KS p = 0.71); one-pair removal flips 141 / 160 / 167 pairs one way; runs with the same seed are bit-identical across thread settings; **item (f) 'would remove it' is PARTIAL as worded:** with a per-pair stream removal and permutation change 0 pairs but flips against a different global seed stay at 142.0 (143.5 for the sequential stream): the array sensitivity goes, the seed noise does not. **(2) PARTIAL:** the counts reproduce exactly (157.8 / 142.3 / 151.8) only under a definition the draft never states (half the symmetric difference); sum p(1 - p) over ten seeds reproduces as 133.4 but is biased low by 11.8% ((n-1)/n gives 148.2; 200 disjoint seeds 151.2); independent-draw flips predicted 151.2 vs 151.3 observed; for the quoted statistic the null SD is 4.1 so 133.5 versus 142.3 is **2.2 SD, not the ~1.3 SD the referee pre-declared in phase 1: two low numbers coinciding, not a confirmation (the PLAN.md correction 81cedd771 inherits the referee's phase-1 error and should be amended)**; pair-calibration line failed at 94.6% against 95%; flips concentrate near the threshold (93.2% within |ln r| <= 0.25; 170x higher within 0.05 than beyond 0.30); 848 trials cut flips to 0.586 of the 212-trial value. **(3) PARTIAL:** SD(gamma-hat)/sigma_fit 0.358 / 0.333 (canonical / alt) on a K = 100 G-only sweep; 0.437 / 0.402 on the calc chat's own seed set (46 of 51 builds pair-identical); 0.383 / 0.357 on 151 pooled builds, so 'about 0.3' is low; N trend canonical +0.126 (-0.016 to +0.269), alt +0.133 (-0.108 to +0.339) not excluded; random 2% or 5% thinning gives 0.36 / 0.27 and a row shuffle of the identical pair set 0.12 / 0.09 (the ratio is an estimator-response level, not proportional to the flipped pairs; the linear-thinning control MU6 failed its magnitude prediction); seed averaging recovers a stable centre (1/sqrt(m)) but the deterministic consensus catalogue sits +0.30 / -0.18 sigma_fit from the seed mean and fails the 0.15 line; fit-only SD 0.17 / 0.14, build-only 0.32 / 0.30 sigma_fit; the frozen build k = 0 sits 1.77 SD below the mean of the 100 builds (canonical). **(4) PARTIAL:** ALL 0.320 / 0.276 and EF-only 0.417 / 0.400 reproduce exactly (G-only 0.389 / 0.220 against 0.405 / 0.223); the EF arm uses the earlier stage-F files as shared inputs; with a per-pair G stream EF-only flips fall from 157 to 8, so the EF-only spread rides on claim (1) and the two are not independent confirmations. **Amendment 18: no sentence refuted and the registered procedure needs no change; recommended wording:** define 'flip' (half the symmetric difference); qualify item (f); the 'roughly 5%' statement: the referee's '5 to 9%' was sqrt(1 + r^2) - 1 with r = SD/sigma_fit and no sigma_sys and should not be quoted as an understatement of sigma_tot; in the draft's sigma_tot convention (sigma_tot = sqrt(sigma_fit^2 + 0.02^2)) the understatement sqrt(1 + B^2) - 1 is 4.7% to 8.1% at DR3's N (r 0.33-0.44) and 2.6% to 4.4% at the preregistered DR4 sigma_fit = 0.019 (a preregistration number the referee cannot check; a constant r carried to DR4's N is an extrapolation), and rev 4's '2-5% at DR4 and 4-8% at DR3's N' is consistent with that arithmetic; do not present the two streams as two independent confirmations; the N-trend sentence should say it comes from thinning one DR3 sample, is canonical-only and is not measured at DR4's N; the primary's exact-array determinism, the flip magnitude and the 'G-only sweep measures sigma_build' statements are confirmed as they stand. MUTATE M1-M7 bite (identical seeds: three builds give one CSV sha256 6fff64d964ebaa72, the frozen reference CSV, zero flips and sigma_build = 0.0 exactly on both footings, against 0.0087 / 0.0058 with distinct seeds; M7 nearly tautological); failed controls kept: the MU6 thinning magnitude, the 95% calibration line, the consensus-catalogue line, the consecutive-block seed-averaging line for alt; the pipeline's synthetic self-test exited 1 for 15 of 50 fit-only seeds (recorded, the catalogue lines still print). Not tested: DR4 itself (released 2 December 2026). In-place re-run by the orchestrator: every results file identical, outputs identical apart from timing and the documented post-hoc ordering. Nothing here is a result about gravity; kappa = 1/2 stays fitted. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG239_dr4_seed_noise_referee/README.md |

| CFG240 | THE BARYON-CALIBRATION WALL AS A THEOREM: Lean module ChainCert/CalibrationWall.lean (50 theorems, namespace CalibrationWall) plus a numeric Fisher companion; criteria 60eaf031 and the 36 phase-1 statements (typechecked with sorry in scratch) committed first in 56b99e337, before any proof or script; the setup is the declared law g_obs = (f g) nu((f g)/a0) with ONE multiplicative calibration f > 0 on g_bar; **Lean certifies the implications from these premises, NOT any empirical fact (that a calibration factor exists, its size or redshift dependence, that a0 is common to the points, that nature follows P2 or nu_mono).** All 36 frozen statements are proved EXACTLY as frozen (each signature occurs verbatim in the module). **T1:** for the deep kernel g_obs = sqrt(f a0 g) exactly, g_obs depends on (f, a0) only through f*a0 (an iff), a drift delta in log f or log a0 moves log g_obs by delta/2; for P2 g_obs^2 = f^2 g^2 + (f a0) g, and for equal products the ratio g_obs'^2/g_obs^2 is exactly (1 + rho^2 y)/(1 + y) (rho = f'/f, y = f g/a0), so the relative difference is at most |1 - rho^2| y and vanishes linearly as y -> 0 (the same limit for any kernel with nu sqrt(y) -> 1 deep). **T2a (the orchestrator's hand derivation, confirmed by hand, sympy and Lean):** for exact P2 any two distinct positive g_bar determine (f, a0) uniquely (hypotheses f, f', a0, a0' > 0, g1, g2 > 0, g1 != g2; no 'not deep' condition); ONE point is not enough even for P2 (witness f' = f/2, a0' = 1.5 f g + 2 a0). **T2b:** the deep kernel factors through f*a0, so two points do not help. **T2c (the wall is a CONDITIONING statement, not a failure of injectivity):** d log g_obs/d log f = (1 + 2y)/(2(1 + y)), d log g_obs/d log a0 = 1/(2(1 + y)), rows (1 - b, b) with b = 1/(2(1 + y)); the determinant is (y1 - y2)/(2(1 + y1)(1 + y2)) = b(y2) - b(y1), valid for any kernel, nonzero iff y1 != y2, below 1/2 in absolute value, tending to 0 when both points are deep or both are Newtonian. **T3:** for any nu -> 1 at large y, g_obs/g -> f and the a0 dependence disappears. **T2e (nu_mono):** passed the frozen 'not formalisable cleanly' test (about 60 lines, standard axioms only; the frozen fallback was not used): injectivity of (f, a0) for nu_mono from two distinct positive g_bar through one kernel-general theorem with the premise of strict convexity of log(e^u nu(e^u)), proved for nu_mono and for P2. **T4 (added at the PAPER38 owner's request, formalised):** for rows (1 - b_i, b_i) with every b_i in [0, 1/2], var(log a0) = (F^-1)_22 >= 9 sigma^2/N, i.e. sigma(log a0) >= 3 sigma/sqrt(N) with f free (T4_design_bound, T4_sigma_floor); the range 0 <= b_i <= 1/2 is a HYPOTHESIS (for nu_mono it is checked numerically, not in Lean) and the equality design (2/3 of points deep, 1/3 Newtonian) is numeric only. Fourteen theorems are additions to the frozen list (T4 on the covariance and 12 helpers). **Library 281 -> 331 theorems:** lake build ChainCert (8783 jobs) succeeds; verify_chain.sh VERIFY: PASS (331 theorems, 0 non-standard axioms, 0 sorry); MUTATE=1 VERIFY: FAIL as required; the Mutate file CalibrationWallMutate.lean.txt has 41 false variants, all 41 rejected (41 errors); ChainCert/Axioms.out, verify_chain.out and verify_chain_MUTATE.out regenerated; registration edits limited to one import in ChainCert.lean, the new #print axioms lines in Axioms.lean and a README append. **NUMERIC COMPANION (CFG240_fisher.py, exit 0, PL1-PL10 pass; MUTATE M1-M6 all bite):** the analytic Fisher entries match finite differences to 4.8e-13 (line 1e-8) and sympy to 5.6e-15; the T4 floor has 0 violations in 19,505 seeded random designs and equality at the two-cluster design to 2e-16; the T1/T3 limits reproduce (P2 equal-product relative difference (rho^2 - 1) y/(1 + y) to 2e-16 with mpmath). **Break-even table** (smallest y_max with sigma(log a0) < 0.1 dex for all larger y_max up to the cap 1e4; machine-readable CFG240_break_even_table.json with a header record and 270 case records; 90 rows in the README between TABLE-BEGIN/TABLE-END; checked equal by PL10): rho is negative in every cell and tends to -1 (about -0.8 and cond(F) about 13 at break-even); the T4 floor means 0.1 dex needs N > 9 at sigma = 0.1 and N > 36 at sigma = 0.2 (so 'none' for N = 5 and for N = 20 at sigma = 0.2); P2, N = 20, sigma = 0.1, no prior: y_min = 1e-3 gives y_max* 5.5, y_min = 0.01 gives 7.9, y_min = 0.1 gives none (best 0.117 at y_max about 48); with a prior on log f of 0.15 dex the y_min = 0.01 value drops to 5.5 (7.2 at 0.3 dex); nu_mono, same N and sigma: y_min = 1e-3 gives 17, y_min = 0.01 has a first crossing at 41.7 but no stable break-even (minimum sigma 0.0958), y_min = 0.1 gives none. **Misses kept:** the frozen estimate 'nu_mono within a factor 2 of P2' was wrong (about 5.3x more y_max at y_min = 0.01); the P2 headline estimate (first break-even about 17, 80% interval 8-40) missed (7.94; the continuum approximation overstated sigma at y_max = 10 by about 8%); PL3b first failed in double precision (1.1e-10 against 1e-12, from cancellation; moved to mpmath, 2e-16, frozen tolerance kept); M2's first implementation did not break PL7 and was fixed; the first main run had PL10 failing because the README table was not yet pasted in. Not covered: non-P2 and non-nu_mono kernels, other systematics, selection. Lean certifies the algebra; it says nothing about whether any survey has such a calibration factor. kappa = 1/2 stays fitted. | campaign_fresh_gravity/CFG240_calibration_wall/README.md; fable_independent_2026/lean_2026/ChainCert/CalibrationWall.lean |
